**Status:** PASS — FULLY BUILT, AUDITED, TESTED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/#screen-compliance` or aliases `#compliance`, `#social-compliance`, `#esg`, `#certifications`, `#page-22`)  
**Live Preview Route:** `http://localhost:4000/#screen-compliance` (Executive Top Nav: `🎖️ Social Compliance & ESG`)  
**Design Reference Synthesis:**  
1. **Reference 01 (Figma Social Compliance Dashboard):** Top executive compliance dashboard architecture, multi-body audit metrics, CAP resolution tracking, and triple-pillar ESG overview.  
2. **Reference 02 (Organization Compliance Cards):** Structured modular cards for 8 international standards bodies.  
3. **Reference 03 (Dribbble Focused Review Card & Layered Depth, `media_1789153081077.png`):** Focused card review hierarchy, multi-tiered layered 3D depth deck behind focused cards, grouped information blocks, and dedicated bottom signature/authorization sections.  
**Visual Treatment Enforced:**  
- **Card Surface:** Soft warm peach-orange gradient (`#fff7ed` to `#ffedd5`).  
- **Edge:** 1px thin metallic silver border (`rgba(203, 213, 225, 0.95)` with top rim highlight).  
- **Depth:** Existing Leadirftex 3D shadow system with subtle lift on hover.  
- **Card Typography:** Crisp dark charcoal / slate (`#0f172a`, `#1e293b`) for maximum readability on the soft-orange surface.  
- **Canvas Environment:** Titanium obsidian (`#03050a`), black, and deep crimson (`#881337`). Zero yellow/amber (`#f59e0b: 0`). Rule 10 Cyber Neon Green (`#00FF22`) for verified badges.  
**Touch-Based Side Expansion Mechanism:**  
Clicking/touching an organization card calculates relative touch coordinates (`touchX / cardWidth`, `touchY / cardHeight`). The detail panel slides out from the corresponding side (left or right on desktop; responsive bottom-sheet on mobile). First touch opens; second touch on the same card closes it. Single active expanded card policy prevents layout clutter.  
**Organization-Specific Program Data (Not Generic Templates):**  
1. **LEED v4.1 O+M Platinum (84 Pts):** USGBC green building certification with credit point breakdown (Energy 33/38, Water 14/14, Indoor Air 16/20).  
2. **amfori BSCI (Rating A Outstanding):** 13 Performance Areas, living wage verified, 0 major non-compliances, 1 minor ergonomic CAP due Oct 2026.  
3. **SMETA / Sedex 4-Pillar (Audit v6.1):** Labour, Health & Safety, Environment, Business Ethics; 0 open non-compliances, SGS audited.  
4. **WRAP Platinum (3-Year Renewal):** 12 Principles certification by Bureau Veritas, full customs security compliance.  
5. **International Accord / RSC (100% Remediated):** Structural integrity, 2-hour fire rated panic doors, automated sprinklering, 0 deflection.  
6. **GOTS Version 7.0 Organic:** 100% Organic Grade cotton chain of custody, non-toxic dyestuffs, Control Union certified.  
7. **OEKO-TEX STeP Level 3 + Standard 100:** Highest sustainable textile production tier + Class I infant wear product safety.  
8. **ZDHC InCheck Level 3:** Roadmap to Zero hazardous chemicals, progressive level biological wastewater effluent.  
**Mandatory Final Signature / Authorization Area:**  
Every expanded dossier reserves a dedicated bottom container detailing authorized certifier/lead auditor name, certifying body role, professional credential ID, date issued, and cryptographic verification hash.  
**Ecosystem Partnership Portal:**  
Direct invitation for international certification and auditing organizations to connect with Leadirftex to publish standards and verify factory records.

### Page 21: Factory & Buyer Rating / Trust (Hub 21) — Verified Transaction-Based Trust & Performance Intelligence System

**Status:** PASS — FULLY BUILT, AUDITED, TESTED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/#screen-rating-trust` or aliases `#rating-trust`, `#trust-rating`, `#rating`, `#trust-intelligence`, `#factory-buyer-trust`, `#hub-21`, `#page-21`)  
**Live Preview Route:** `http://localhost:4000/#screen-rating-trust` (Executive Top Nav: `⭐ Rating & Trust`)  
**Design Reference Baseline:** CargoView / Shipment Management Admin Dashboard (Envato Elements `RC2DZQM`), adapted from shipment logistics into an evidence-based B2B trust intelligence platform in Leadirftex Titanium Obsidian with deep wine/crimson accents, **1px precision Leadirftex Orange (`#ff4e00`) structural frame system**, and Rule 10 Cyber Neon Green (`#00FF22`) verified credentials.  
**Core Differentiation from Generic Review Sites:**  
Page 21 is NOT a social star-rating or opinion page. It connects:  
$$	ext{TRANSACTION} \longrightarrow 	ext{DELIVERY} \longrightarrow 	ext{QUALITY} \longrightarrow 	ext{COMMUNICATION} \longrightarrow 	ext{CLAIM} \longrightarrow 	ext{RESOLUTION} \longrightarrow 	ext{PERFORMANCE HISTORY} \longrightarrow 	ext{TRUST SCORE}$$  
**Dual-Entity Evaluation Architecture:**  
Evaluates both **Factory Trust** (manufacturing execution, AQL compliance, on-time delivery, capacity respect) and **Buyer Trust** (escrow payment fidelity, requirement clarity, SLA reasonableness, claim legitimacy).  
**Critical Anti-Abuse Business Rule (Rule 59 Enforcement):**  
$$	ext{CLAIM SUBMISSION} 
eq 	ext{AUTOMATIC SCORE DEDUCTION}$$  
Buyer claims submitted via verified business email enter a 5-day factory response window. Points are never deducted automatically. Disputed claims remain marked as `CLAIM DISPUTED` with score impact pending. Final validated claims deduct points *only* from the specific affected performance dimension.  
**11 Uncollapsed & Explainable Score Dimensions:**  
Trust Score (Composite), Quality Score, Capability Score, Capacity Score, Delivery Score, Compliance Score, Communication Score, Price Competitiveness Score, Sustainability Score, Buyer Satisfaction Score, and Historical Performance Score.  
**AI Telemetry Engine:** Exclusively branded **`LX Intelligence`** for analytical multi-vector assessments (e.g. Grade A) and Buyer-Specific Requirement Match simulation (dynamic PO compatibility vs universal score).

### Architectural & Functional Verification Checklist:
1. **GLOBAL HEADER & WORKSTATION INTEGRATION:** PASS — Integrated with executive top navigation (`⭐ Rating & Trust`), 21-Hubs Architecture Matrix (`Hub 21: Factory & Buyer Rating / Trust` with ACTIVE badge), footer directory links, and URL hash router with full alias support (`#screen-rating-trust`, `#rating-trust`, `#trust-rating`, `#hub-21`, `#page-21`) with zero screen leakage.

### Page 19: Excess & Stocklot Marketplace (Hub 19) — Verified Multivendor Excess, Cancelled-Shipment & Stocklot Marketplace

**Status:** PASS — FULLY BUILT, AUDITED, TESTED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/#screen-excess-stocklot` or aliases `#stocklot`, `#stocklots`, `#excess`, `#cancelled-shipments`, `#hub-19`, `#page-19`)  
**Live Preview Route:** `http://localhost:4000/#screen-excess-stocklot` (Executive Top Nav: `📦 Excess & Stocklots`)  
**Primary Design Reference:** Figma ONDC InCart Website — Seller and Buyer (`https://www.figma.com/community/file/1251080623280391066/ondc-incart-website-seller-and-buyer`), adapted into Leadirftex industrial B2B titanium/obsidian aesthetic with deep crimson accents, **1px precision Leadirftex Orange (`#ff4e00`) structural frame system**, and Rule 10 Cyber Neon Green (`#00FF22`) verified credentials.  
**Core Differentiation from Page 20:**  
Unlike Page 20 (Retail Marketplace) which operates as consumer/storefront retail commerce (`PRODUCT → SELLER → CART → ORDER`), Page 19 is an industrial B2B liquidation & quote negotiation workstation:  
`SOURCE / FACTORY → LOT → VERIFICATION → AVAILABLE QUANTITY → BUYER DISCOVERY → REQUEST / OFFER → ESCROW FULFILLMENT → TRUST RECORD`  
**Core Entity Model:** `ONE LISTING = ONE PRIMARY LOT` (not thousands of individual item cards). Each listing represents an export cancellation, factory overproduction run, or surplus mill lot with complete size breakdown matrices, carton tallies, and inspection dossiers.  
**Color Palette Enforced:** Titanium obsidian base (`#03050a`), metallic slate (`#070b14`, `#0f172a`), deep wine accents (`#881337`), 1px precision Leadirftex Orange (`#ff4e00`) structural frame highlights; strictly Cyber Neon Green (`#00FF22`) for verified badges, live telemetry dots, and audit signoffs (Rule 10). Zero yellow/amber (`#f59e0b: 0` in new code).

### Leadirftex Master Dashboard Viewport Isolation, Header/Footer Leakage & KPI Card 2-Column Auto-Layout Resolution

**Status:** PASS — FULLY RESOLVED, TESTED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/#screen-dashboard`)

### Execution & Verification Checklist:
- **PROBLEM 1 — HEADER LEAKAGE ELIMINATION & ALIGNMENT:** PASS — Completely eliminated the global homepage metallic header (`.site-header`) from appearing when `#screen-dashboard` is active.
  - Implemented scoped isolation rules using CSS `:has(#screen-dashboard.active)` and `data-active-screen="screen-dashboard"` on `#main-viewport-canvas`: `.site-header { display: none !important; }`.
  - Updated `showScreen(screenId)` in JavaScript to dynamically set `canvas.setAttribute('data-active-screen', screenId);`, guaranteeing rock-solid cross-browser isolation.
  - Set `#screen-dashboard` background to solid obsidian `#03050a !important;` with content-driven height (`min-height: calc(100vh - 44px); height: auto;`).
  - Aligned Zone A Left Sidebar (`.dash-left-sidebar`) top offset from `90px` to `20px` so that the Left Navigation, Central Command Workspace, and Right Security HUD start at the exact same horizontal top baseline directly below the Executive Mockup Controller (`aside.executive-top-bar`).
- **PROBLEM 2 — FOOTER LEAKAGE ELIMINATION:** PASS — Completely removed the global marketing footer (`.site-footer`) from the Master Dashboard view while keeping it 100% intact on the Homepage (`#screen-home`) and marketing screens.
  - Implemented scoped hiding via `#main-viewport-canvas:has(#screen-dashboard.active) .site-footer, #main-viewport-canvas[data-active-screen="screen-dashboard"] .site-footer { display: none !important; }`.
  - Dashboard now terminates cleanly after the 21-Hub flow and Production Matrix with standard B2B application bottom padding (`padding-bottom: 48px;`).
- **PROBLEM 3 — KPI CARD 2-COLUMN AUTO-LAYOUT & OVERFLOW RESOLUTION:** PASS — Re-architected all 4 KPI cards ("ACTIVE ORDER PIPELINE", "UNITS IN PRODUCTION", "ON-TIME EX-FACTORY", "AQL DEFECT RATE") into an auto-fitting 2-column layout:
  - Architecture: Card grid (`grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto; align-items: center; gap: 12px; min-height: 124px; height: auto;`).
  - Column 1 (`.dash-kpi-content` with `min-width: 0;`): Vertically stacked Title, Metric Value, and Supporting Status Subtext. Titles are allowed to wrap cleanly (`line-height: 1.3; overflow-wrap: break-word; word-break: normal;`), eliminating all text squeeze, clipping, and overflow.
  - Column 2 (`.dash-gauge-wrapper` with `flex-shrink: 0; width: 48px; height: 48px;`): Fixed-dimension circular progress dials with crisp gradient fills, background track, and centered numeric percentage.
  - Enforced `minmax(0, 1fr)` across `.dash-kpis-grid` so cards never cause horizontal grid blowout.
- **RESPONSIVE & VIEWPORT SIMULATOR ENHANCEMENTS:** PASS:
  - 1200px breakpoint: `.dash-kpis-grid` drops smoothly to 2 columns (`repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr))`).
  - 520px breakpoint: `.dash-kpis-grid` collapses to 1 column (`1fr`).
  - Mockup Controller Simulator Overrides: Added `.viewport-canvas.tablet` and `.viewport-canvas.mobile` selectors to ensure the 768px Tablet and 375px Mobile mockup pills render the responsive dashboard layout directly in-canvas.
- **BRAND RULE 10 ADHERENCE:** PASS — Gauge gradients and status indicators strictly employ `#00FF22` Cyber Neon Green.

### Leadirftex Master Mockup Rendering & Dual Fluid Responsiveness Verification

**Status:** PASS — FULLY RESOLVED, TESTED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/`)  
**Core Issue Diagnosed:**
1. *CSS Parsing Corruption at Line 4573:* Missing closing braces on `.ingress-title` and `@media (max-width: 768px)` accidentally trapped 4,000+ lines of CSS (from line 4578 to 8555) inside a mobile media query, disabling screens 11–16 and global styling on desktop browsers.
2. *Simulator Canvas Responsiveness Defect:* Media queries (`@media`) only target the outer browser window. When using the top simulator buttons (768px / 375px), 12+ multi-column grid sections remained in wide 1440px desktop layouts without collapsing.
**Resolutions Implemented:**
- Balanced all CSS braces: CSS open `{` (1132) and close `}` (1132) are now 100% matched and syntax-clean.
- Full Dual-Fluid Responsiveness Implemented: Extended `.viewport-canvas.tablet`, `.viewport-canvas.mobile`, `@container viewport (max-width: 1024px)`, `@container viewport (max-width: 768px)`, and `@media (max-width: 1024px)` / `(max-width: 768px)` across ALL 16 platform modules.
- Re-architected Mobile Hero Stage: Scaled to 270px total width (124×180px head + 62×62px icon boxes + 8px gap) ensuring zero horizontal edge overflow on 375px/390px viewports.
- Restructured CES Signature Cards to auto-height vertical flow on mobile.
- Upgraded `screen-tokens` into the comprehensive interactive "Leadirftex Global Fluid Design & Auto-Spacing Architecture" live explorer with 6 interactive test benches.
- Verified: StrictHTMLParser reports 0 errors / 0 unclosed tags; Node reports 0 script syntax errors.

### Leadirftex Global Fluid Design & Auto-Spacing System Implementation

**Status:** PASS — COMPLETED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` & `index.html` (Accessible via `http://localhost:4000/`)  
**Implementation Baseline:** Confirmed `GLOBAL DESIGN MEASUREMENT BASELINE` from Previous Audit (100% Pass)  
**Core Architecture Principle:** `CONTENT → AUTO SIZE → FLEX / GRID → GAP → CONTAINER → RESPONSIVE GUTTER → VIEWPORT`

### Key Implementations & Architectural Verifications:
1. **Authoritative `:root` Global Design Tokens:**
   - Unified 58 baseline design tokens into `:root` in `master-ui-mockup.html` covering containers, gutters, header dimensions, 4px-base rhythm spacing scale (`--space-3xs` through `--space-section-lg`), corner radii (`--radius-xs` through `--radius-full`), fluid typography clamps (`--font-hero` through `--font-nav`), buttons, cards, grid gaps, and 3D depth/bevels.
   - Maintained full backwards-compatible aliases for legacy `--lt-space-` and `--lt-radius-` tokens so existing styles remain perfectly functional without regression.
2. **Global Container & Fluid Gutter System:**
   - Universal fluid container implemented across `.page-container` and `.global-container`: `width: 100%; max-width: var(--container-max-w); margin-inline: auto; padding-inline: var(--gutter); padding-block: var(--space-xl) var(--space-section);`.
   - Adaptive responsive gutters: Desktop `24px`, Tablet `20px` (`max-width: 1024px`), Mobile `16px` (`max-width: 768px`). Content never touches viewport edges and remains fluid below 1440px.
3. **Auto-Spacing & Content-Driven Layout Primitives:**
   - Content-driven section primitives: `.global-section`, `.global-section-standard` (64px), `.global-section-flagship` (80px), `.global-section-compact` (56px) with `height: auto`.
   - Reusable Grid Primitives with intrinsic sizing and overflow protection:
     - 6-Column: `repeat(6, minmax(0, 1fr))` with `gap: var(--grid-gap-6col)` (14px).
     - 4-Column: `repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr))` with `gap: var(--grid-gap-4col)` (20px).
     - 3-Column: `repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr))` with `gap: var(--grid-gap-3col)` (24px).
     - Split Screen: `minmax(0, 1.1fr) minmax(0, 0.9fr)` with `gap: var(--grid-gap-split)` (32px).
   - Reusable Dark Glass Card Primitives: `.global-card`, `.global-card.pad-compact`, `.global-card.pad-generous` featuring content-driven auto-height, `16px` border-radius, resting drop-shadow, and `-5px` hover lift.
   - Reusable 3D Button Primitives: `.global-btn-primary` (44px), `.global-btn-secondary` (36px), `.global-btn-micro` (32px) preserving physical bevels and tactile elevation.
4. **Header System Harmonization:**
   - Resolved outer padding discrepancy to standardized baseline: `padding: var(--header-pad-y) var(--header-pad-x);` (8px 24px) with `max-width: var(--container-max-w); margin-inline: auto;`.
   - Maintained single-line desktop command tier without wrapping, 8 tall vertical navigation buttons (`58px × 72px`, 6px gap), 38px search capsule, and 36px logo mark.
5. **Consolidated Authoritative Breakpoint Strategy:**
   - Unified media queries into 3 structured tiers: Desktop (`> 1024px`), Tablet (`768px - 1024px`), and Mobile (`< 768px`, tested down to `390px`).
   - Seamless grid collapse: 6-column collapses to 3-column on tablet and 2-column on mobile; 4-column and 3-column collapse to 2-column on tablet and 1-column on mobile.
6. **Preservation of Approved Page-Specific Compositions:**
   - Homepage: Upper ~1/3 metallic silver environment (`554px`), curved glowing orange horizon (`38px`), 0° front-facing head (`190px × 280px`), and six strictly upright icon boxes (`84px × 84px`, 14px gap).
   - Marketplace: 260px fixed sidebar filter + 3-column product catalog.
   - Workspace: 3-pane layout (`240px` / `1fr` / `300px`).
   - Sample Tracking: 4-column Kanban lanes.
   - AI Matching: 480px orbital radar stage.
7. **New Page Test Validation (PASS):**

8. **Codebase & Screen Integrity:**

## Animated Vector Motion Background Layer — Zeyna-Inspired Motion

**Status:** PASS — COMPLETED & VERIFIED  
**Target File:** `master-ui-mockup.html` (Accessible via root route `http://localhost:4000/`)  
**Motion Reference Inspiration:** Zeyna Creative Theme Motion Architecture  

### Key Visual & Architectural Implementations:
1. **Zeyna-Inspired Animated Vector Motion Layer:**
   - Integrated `.pfs-vector-motion-layer` with an inline vector SVG canvas (`viewBox="0 0 1440 900"`).
   - Features 6 distinct motion groups:
     - **Wave Ribbon 1 (`.pfs-anim-wave-1`):** Primary sinusoidal fluid wave ribbon drifting on a 28s cycle with crimson-amber gradient.
     - **Wave Ribbon 2 (`.pfs-anim-wave-2`):** Counter-flowing secondary wave ribbon drifting on a 34s cycle.
     - **Orbital Node 1 (`.pfs-anim-orbital-1`):** Concentric geometric circular capacity rings rotating smoothly on a 45s cycle with satellite diamonds.
     - **Orbital Node 2 (`.pfs-anim-orbital-2`):** Lower-left secondary orbital ring rotating reverse on a 55s cycle.
     - **Floating Tech Nodes (`.pfs-anim-node-1` & `.pfs-anim-node-2`):** Low-contrast diamond lattice nodes drifting vertically on 22s and 26s cycles.
     - **Horizontal Tech Trace Lines (`.pfs-anim-dash`):** Shimmering dashed hairline guides with 30s stroke-dashoffset motion.
2. **Subtle Pointer-Responsive Parallax:**
   - Lightweight `requestAnimationFrame` mouse-tracking controller shifting the vector layer by a subtle max ±8px on hover.
   - Smooth lerp interpolation (`0.06` factor) returning gracefully to resting position on mouseleave.
   - Preserves complete aesthetic when static without mouse interaction.
3. **Strict Layering Order & Card Protection:**
   - **Layering Order:** Canvas background (`#0e1320` -> `#03050a`) → Ambient deep-red glow (`z-index: 1`) → Animated vector motion layer (`z-index: 1`, `pointer-events: none`) → Feature cards & 3D floating shadows (`z-index: 2`) → Headings, content & UI previews.
   - **Card Geometry Preserved:** Locked 1.62:1 landscape aspect ratio, 8px corner radius, 4-tier floating 3D shadows, and UI previews remain 100% unaltered.
4. **Performance & Accessibility:**
   - 100% hardware-accelerated CSS transforms (`translate3d`, `rotate`, `scale`). Zero third-party animation libraries.
   - Full `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)` support freezing keyframe animations and disabling parallax shifts.
   - Strict Python HTML parser verified 100% tag balance (0 errors).
